## Executive Recommendation

- Keep the exact half-open target range and current direct class owner. No split, merge, new child, no-owner disposition, or emitter reroute is warranted.
- Correct both callback identities from base-slot contracts, not from descriptive behavior alone.
- Use `controlIndex` for the first `0x46e8f0` argument. `controlIndex == 1` is the dialog control-list index selected by `DialogPane::OnControlCommand`, not the resource/control id `30`; the old constructor creates the close control in the corresponding ordered control list.
- Use `Event *` and `bool` for `0x46e930`; preserve its physical secondary-view ABI as `this == complete object + 0xa0` in IDA while expressing the source method normally on `WebBoardDialogOld`.
- Declare the complete `WebBoardDialogOld` derived tail in UID0000G3 H: `BrowserControlPane *m_browserControlPane` followed by eight bytes of opaque retained storage. The opaque disposition is required by the exact `0x278` extent and exhaustive no-access result; it must not be mislabeled alignment padding or silently omitted.
- Replace `GetBrowserWindow()` with direct `m_browserHostHwnd` access in both `GetClientRect` and `MoveWindow`. Require the included BrowserControlPane declaration to expose that existing field to this source; do not add a new inline or out-of-line accessor absent from current evidence.
- Use the best source-facing names `OnBrowserDialogClosed`, `OnBrowserDialogOpened`, `ShutdownBrowserWindow`, `OnDestroy`, `OnHide`, `QueuePaneForDeferredDeletion`, `GetScreenBounds`, and `RenderPresentation`; do not retain `sub_`, address-bearing, `a1/a2`, or decompiler-shaped names in formal source. `OnBrowserDialogClosed` is the directly consumed primary `+0x68` hook; `OnBrowserDialogOpened` is the best lifecycle-pair inference for the adjacent uncalled code-folded primary `+0x6c` hook.
- All accepted ordinary documentation/formal changes were applied and scoped-validated after the earlier Gate 1 callback authorization. Supervisor IDA Gate 2B, manual coverage rows/validators, autogen refresh, and current generated semantic readbacks are complete and reconciled below. Fresh exact-artifact Gate 1 and the still-open combined-Gate/lifecycle rows remain supervisor-owned.

## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

1. **Primary callback contract:** UID0001QV/UID00001N current `DialogPane` evidence places `OnControlCommand(int,int)` at primary `+0x48`. Target cell `0x006136d4` is exactly primary `+0x48`; therefore `0x46e8f0` is this override. `OnCloseStateChanged` is rejected.
2. **First argument meaning:** `sub [ebp+arg_0],1` and `retn 8` prove two 32-bit stack arguments and a branch on the first. `DialogPane::OnControlCommand` defines those as `controlIndex` and `notifyCode`. The value `1` is a control-list index. The prior report's comparison with resource id `30` confused two separate identifier domains.
3. **Secondary callback contract:** the current EventHandler secondary slot `+0x04` is `HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *)`. Target cell `0x00613704` is exactly that slot. The current IDA type already identifies `Event *`; `InputEvent` and `OnInputEventWithBrowserReposition` are rejected.
4. **Secondary `this` shape:** target code reads `secondary+0x168`, normalizing to complete `+0x208` `m_isTitleDragActive`; reads `secondary+0x1cc`, normalizing to complete `+0x26c` `m_browserControlPane`; and invokes primary slot `+0x28` through `secondary-0xa0`. This proves one complete `WebBoardDialogOld` with an EventHandler view at `+0xa0`.
5. **Destructor source boundary:** the source-authored destructor effect is the singleton clear. Three BrowserPane vptr stores and the tail base destructor are MSVC lowering. The separate scalar wrapper/adjustors and vtable deletion cells are also generated, not handwritten destructor source.
6. **Close-command sequence:** when `controlIndex == 1`, exact order is primary `+0x68`, `BrowserControlPane::ShutdownBrowserWindow(m_browserControlPane)`, primary `+0x38 OnDestroy`, primary `+0x40 OnHide`, then `BlackHole::QueuePaneForDeferredDeletion(g_pApplicationCleanupQueue,this)`. No null check and no `m_browserControlPane = NULL` store occur.
7. **Anonymous `+0x68` name:** BrowserPane-derived dialog close handlers consistently invoke the same code-folded no-op base slot immediately before browser shutdown and dialog teardown. `OnBrowserDialogClosed()` is the strongest human name. Exact spelling lacks symbols, but the role is resolved well enough to reject raw-slot syntax in source.
8. **Pointer/mouse order:** the binary reads `event->m_type` and drag state before calling the base on the early-return path, and calls the base before geometry work on the active-drag path. A single unconditional base call hoisted above the condition could change behavior and is rejected.
9. **No null guards:** event, active browser pane, embedded browser pane, screen pane, and cleanup queue are dereferenced/called without checks on the observed paths. Existing draft guards change behavior and must be removed.
10. **Hosted-window geometry:** `GetClientRect` uses `g_activeBrowserControlPane->m_browserHostHwnd` at `+0x11c`; `GetScreenBounds` supplies the complete dialog bounds; `MoveWindow` uses `m_browserControlPane->m_browserHostHwnd` at `+0x11c`, left/top plus 30, active client width/height, and repaint false. These are the browser-host child handles; they remain distinct from unrelated ordinary pane/window handles elsewhere in the object family.
11. **Screen update:** current `ScreenPane` documentation declares `RenderPresentation()`, and IDA names `0x00557140` `ScreenPane_RenderPresentation`. `g_pScreenPane->RenderPresentation()` is therefore stronger than the stale `Refresh()` spelling even though the broader UID00039P file owner remains `Surface`.
12. **Ownership/source placement:** class-specific vtables, singleton clear, embedded old browser control, fixed old geometry, constructor resources, and adjacent packet slot all support UID0000G3 under UID0000P9. `DialogPane` is a base dependency, Browser is infrastructure, and RankingDialog is stale generated pollution.
13. **Range:** all executable bytes in the target belong to the three methods; all internal/external gaps are `0xcc`. No mixed data/helper ownership exists inside the page.
14. **CPP/H:** method definitions belong in UID000212 CPP. Class declarations belong in UID0000G3 H. UID000212 H remains blank to prevent duplicate declarations. UID0000G3 CPP should retain only `[[CHILDREN]]`.
15. **Score blockers:** the prior callback-name/signature, null-guard, helper-name, source-channel, and current-IDB raw-name blockers have concrete resolutions or exact Gate 2B actions. The target is not capped at its prior score by any unattempted investigation.
16. **Complete derived layout:** live constructor assembly stores only the embedded browser-control pointer at `this+0x26c`; caller windows `0x508db6-0x508ddd` and `0x5145fc-0x51461e` each allocate exactly `0x278` before calling the constructor. Since current BrowserPane evidence fixes the base extent at `0x26c`, the old derived tail is exactly twelve bytes: the proven pointer `[+0x26c,+0x270)` plus retained storage `[+0x270,+0x278)`.
17. **Tail semantic investigation:** MCP `search_text` over `[0x46e260,0x46efe0)` for `(26Ch|270h|274h)` returned eight exact `+0x26c` instructions and zero `+0x270/+0x274` instructions. Those eight cover the raw teardown, active layout helper, old constructor, old close callback, and old response path. The class-specific old source island, scalar wrapper/adjustors, constructor callers, current class/file/constructor docs, and repository searches provide no read, write, address-taking use, initializer, destructor effect, RTTI subobject, or named declaration for the final eight bytes. They are not alignment padding because a four-byte pointer ends at aligned `0x270` while allocation continues eight bytes. The strongest current first-draft disposition is `unsigned char m_reserved[8]`; exact original semantics are exhaustively unavailable from current artifacts and cap final-source confidence below 95.
18. **Hosted-HWND API investigation:** bounded MCP function lookups for `GetBrowserWindow`, `BrowserControlPane_GetBrowserWindow`, and `BrowserControlPane::GetBrowserWindow` returned not found; an IDA names query returned zero rows; current by-* and repository searches likewise found no declaration. The target instructions at `0x46e970` and `0x46e9ad/0x46e9b3` directly load `BrowserControlPane+0x11c`, matching current UID000015 field `HWND m_browserHostHwnd`. Direct member access is therefore selected, and UID000015 must preserve an accessible field declaration rather than gaining an invented accessor. One optional whole-image rendered-listing search timed out at 60 seconds; all required bounded queries succeeded, and the immediate `idb_list` readback is retained as a dated healthy-worker snapshot rather than permanent active-session authority.
19. **BrowserPane hook declaration route:** BrowserPane primary cells `0x613354/0x613358`, active WebBoard cells `0x613644/0x613648`, and old WebBoard cells `0x6136f4/0x6136f8` all contain `0x0041b6a0`; that one-byte `retn` function is currently named `@_guard_check_icall_nop@4` because 682 unrelated code/data references were folded to it. Only primary `+0x68` is called by the three Browser-dialog close sequences. `OnBrowserDialogClosed()` is therefore the direct evidence-backed `+0x68` declaration. No Browser-family call to `+0x6c` was found; adjacency and identical empty implementation make `OnBrowserDialogOpened()` the best human lifecycle-pair inference for first-draft source, below final lexical confidence but superior to an unnamed/raw slot.
20. **Header route:** UID000015 and UID000019 both emit through UID0000HV into sibling `NexusTK/browser/Browser.h`. UID000015 supplies `#pragma once`, Windows/COM and `../ui/core/ControlPane.h`/`../ui/core/DialogPane.h` includes plus the complete BrowserControlPane declaration; UID000019 supplies the BrowserPane declaration and no data members beyond DialogPane. UID0000G2 and UID0000G3 emit through `NexusTK/ui/dialogs/WebBoardDialog.h` and include `../../browser/Browser.h`. This route makes `m_browserHostHwnd`, `ShutdownBrowserWindow`, both BrowserPane hooks, and the base types visible without including a `.cpp` file or inventing an accessor.
21. **Active UID00020Y closure:** live functions `[0x46d330,0x46d359)`, `[0x46d360,0x46d3a5)`, and `[0x46d3b0,0x46d475)` have SHA256 identities `A7050E69D8145C5C0AA31C6FDFDA1FFF982C20F24EBE3186977E97C5D7FD840C`, `044AA7857F3FC2357803D18EB370CDEAD5ABDD98FEFA097E4B4E66CE23983E8B`, and `AC13130D625FCD5B9D3F7468FB410469A6FFC13A2D801DB683BA95BDC421CF2C`. Active primary cell `0x613624` and secondary cell `0x613654` prove the same inherited contracts as old, with exact active differences `controlIndex==0`, `m_browserControlPane=NULL`, and `+11,+42`. UID00020Y is consequently a required same-callback support repair, not future work.

## Evidence Standards Used

- Direct binary authority: live IDB function boundaries, assembly, decompilation, callers/callees, xrefs, bytes, vtable cells, RTTI/COL routes, item heads, types, comments, stack frames, and collision searches.
- Structural authority: canonical `DialogPane` primary/secondary slot maps, class declarations, object-offset normalization, destructor wrapper/adjustor routes, and neighboring packet callback identity.
- Corroborating documentation: current target, class, file, global/storage, active counterpart, browser-control, screen, cleanup-queue, and mixed-vtable pages.
- Negative evidence: no ordinary destructor xref, no direct callback callers beyond vtable cells, no null checks/member clear in the old close body, no mixed ownership inside the exact range, and no collision for each proposed source-facing IDA name.
- Evidence ladder result: exact behavior/contracts are direct facts; human lexical names are selected only after slot/family/context corroboration; no score is based solely on a historical report or generated source.

## Positive Evidence Summary

- Three modeled functions exactly fill the target's executable subranges; all separators and neighboring gaps are verified `0xcc` alignment.
- Primary vtable cell `0x006136d4` and canonical primary `+0x48` contract prove `OnControlCommand(int,int)`.
- Secondary vtable cell `0x00613704` and canonical EventHandler `+0x04` contract prove `HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *)`.
- The ordinary destructor, scalar wrapper, primary/secondary/tertiary vtables, RTTI locators, and adjustors all converge on one `WebBoardDialogOld` object and one source virtual destructor.
- Exact field normalization connects secondary `+0x168/+0x1cc` to complete `m_isTitleDragActive +0x208` and `m_browserControlPane +0x26c`.
- BrowserPane's exact `0x26c` extent, two exact `0x278` constructor allocations, the constructor's sole `+0x26c` pointer store, and zero old-family `+0x270/+0x274` accesses account for every derived byte without pretending the final eight bytes are alignment.
- Exact instructions read both hosted HWNDs directly at BrowserControlPane `+0x11c`; live symbol/name and repository declaration searches find no `GetBrowserWindow()` API.
- Exact globals and call targets explain singleton lifetime, browser HWND sizing, dialog bounds, screen presentation, and deferred deletion without raw names in source.
- Active `WebBoardDialog` provides close/reposition family corroboration while its different control gate, member clear, and `+11,+42` geometry prove the old body must remain separate and exact.
- Live active vtable cells `0x613624/0x613654`, exact function hashes, and the active `0x37c` layout prove UID00020Y can be corrected in the same accepted support callback; no behavioral or declaration blocker remains.
- BrowserPane primary `+0x68/+0x6c` cells for BrowserPane, active, and old all point to one code-folded one-byte return function. The three close paths call `+0x68`; the adjacent uncalled `+0x6c` receives a best-guess lifecycle-pair declaration rather than a raw slot.
- The literal shared header route is now closed: UID000015/UID000019 assemble `NexusTK/browser/Browser.h`; UID0000G2/UID0000G3 include it from `NexusTK/ui/dialogs/WebBoardDialog.h`.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

**1. UID0000G3 WebBoardDialogOld under UID0000P9 WebBoardDialog**

- Evidence for: exact class RTTI/vtables, old singleton lifecycle, old constructor/resources, complete-object offset normalization, embedded browser control, fixed old geometry, adjacent packet callback, and established source tree.
- Evidence against: exact original lexical spelling for the anonymous `+0x68` hook is not symbol-proven.
- Decision: accepted direct owner/emitter and source file. The lexical caveat does not weaken ownership or behavior.

**2. DialogPane/BrowserPane base ownership**

- Evidence for: target overrides inherited slots and calls base/lifecycle methods.
- Evidence against: target cells live in derived old-dialog vtables and bodies use derived singleton/member/geometry state.
- Decision: reject direct ownership; retain as base-contract dependencies.

**3. Browser, RankingDialog, or global/resource ownership**

- Evidence for: BrowserControlPane/ScreenPane/BlackHole globals are dependencies; old generated output polluted RankingDialog.
- Evidence against: consumer dependencies and stale generated labels do not override class-specific vtables, singleton, resources, or source-family placement.
- Decision: reject all as direct owners. No new source file/grouping is needed.

## Negative Evidence Summary

- No direct ordinary-destructor xref does not mean dead code; all deletion cells route through the scalar wrapper and adjustors that recreate ordinary destructor effects.
- No code caller reaches either callback directly; their exact vtable cells are the intended reachability route.
- No null tests exist for event, active browser control, embedded browser control, screen pane, or cleanup queue on the corresponding observed paths.
- No store clears `m_browserControlPane` in `0x46e8f0`; importing the active variant's clear would be a behavioral error.
- No old-family instruction in the bounded implementation island reads, writes, or takes the address of `+0x270` or `+0x274`; no current declaration or archived lead identifies their semantics. Typed field inventions are rejected, but omitting the bytes or labeling them alignment is also rejected.
- No current IDA function/name or by-*/repository declaration supports `GetBrowserWindow()`. The two target HWND loads are direct `+0x11c` member reads, so accessor syntax is rejected.
- Resource id `30` does not explain the `1` gate; canonical `OnControlCommand` semantics and control-list ordering do.
- `OnCloseStateChanged`, `OnInputEventWithBrowserReposition`, `InputEvent *` as the pointer/mouse-callback parameter, `HideDialog`, `ReleaseDialogControls`, `GetPaneRect`, `Rect`, `Refresh`, `Shutdown`, and all raw IDA labels are rejected for that callback source where stronger exact/current names exist. This does not alter the independently documented `WebBoardDialog::OnKeyEvent(InputEvent *)` contract.
- Vtable/COL/adjustor/scalar-wrapper identities are compiler ABI and must not be converted into handwritten source objects or renamed as ordinary class methods.

```cpp
WebBoardDialogOld::~WebBoardDialogOld()
{
    g_pWebBoardDialogOld = NULL;
}

void WebBoardDialogOld::OnControlCommand(int controlIndex, int notifyCode)
{
    (void)notifyCode;

    if (controlIndex != 1)
        return;

    OnBrowserDialogClosed();
    m_browserControlPane->ShutdownBrowserWindow();
    OnDestroy();
    OnHide();
    g_pApplicationCleanupQueue->QueuePaneForDeferredDeletion(this);
}

bool WebBoardDialogOld::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *event)
{
    if (event->m_type != 0 || !m_isTitleDragActive)
        return DialogPane::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(event);

    const bool handled = DialogPane::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(event);

    RECT browserClient;
    ::GetClientRect(g_activeBrowserControlPane->m_browserHostHwnd, &browserClient);

    RectBounds dialogBounds;
    GetScreenBounds(&dialogBounds);

    ::MoveWindow(m_browserControlPane->m_browserHostHwnd,
                 dialogBounds.left + 30,
                 dialogBounds.top + 30,
                 browserClient.right - browserClient.left,
                 browserClient.bottom - browserClient.top,
                 FALSE);

    g_pScreenPane->RenderPresentation();
    return handled;
}
```

WebBoardDialog::~WebBoardDialog()

void WebBoardDialog::OnControlCommand(int controlIndex, int notifyCode)
{
    (void)notifyCode;

    if (controlIndex != 0)
        return;

    OnBrowserDialogClosed();
    m_browserControlPane->ShutdownBrowserWindow();
    m_browserControlPane = NULL;
    OnDestroy();
    OnHide();
    g_pApplicationCleanupQueue->QueuePaneForDeferredDeletion(this);
}

bool WebBoardDialog::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *event)
{
    if (event->m_type != 0 || !m_isTitleDragActive)
        return DialogPane::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(event);

    const bool handled = DialogPane::HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(event);

    RECT browserClient;
    ::GetClientRect(g_activeBrowserControlPane->m_browserHostHwnd, &browserClient);

    RectBounds dialogBounds;
    GetScreenBounds(&dialogBounds);

    ::MoveWindow(m_browserControlPane->m_browserHostHwnd,
                 dialogBounds.left + 11,
                 dialogBounds.top + 42,
                 browserClient.right - browserClient.left,

}
